    require_block_start_enactment_phases(state)
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

state = '//! This module provides the [`State`] — an in-memory representation of the current blockchain state.\n#![allow(clipp...irect_home_transition};\nuse direct_homes::{ensure_dataspace_classes_preserved, ensure_homed_dataspaces_keep_lanes};\n'

    def require_block_start_enactment_phases(state: str) -> None:
        """Bind original ordered start ownership to bounded borrowed phase helpers."""
        state_path = "crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs"
        wrapper = compact_rust(mask_rust(rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )))
        delegate = (
            "self.block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier("
            "curr_block,None,before_start,after_start)"
        )
        if wrapper.count(delegate) != 1 or not wrapper.endswith("{" + delegate + "}"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must retain their original carrier delegate")
        constructor = rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )
        phases = section(
            constructor,
            "        // Height-trigger: open/close referenda at scheduled heights",
            "        let current_slot =",
            state_path,
        )
        # This small statement region contains only the original height and the
        # four unconditional calls: private-settlement expiry, the SCCP begin-block
        # step (specs/sccp.md S0, before the governance pass), Parliament enactment,
        # then SCCP G4 fast-pause lapse cleanup (§4.7.2, §4.14.7). Ignore line
        # comments, but reject hidden conditions,
        # substituted owners/heights, duplicate calls, or swapped execution order.
        phases = re.sub(r"//[^\n]*", "", phases)
        expected = (
            "let now_h = sb._curr_block.height().get();"
            "Self::apply_block_start_private_settlement_expiry(&mut sb, now_h)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::hook::begin_block(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "Self::apply_block_start_parliament_enactments(&mut sb, now_h)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::pause_lifecycle::lapse_after_enactments(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
        )
        if re.sub(r"\s+", "", phases) != re.sub(r"\s+", "", expected):
>           raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must use the original block and height in order")
E           RuntimeError: crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs: start phases must use the original block and height in order

scripts/tests/check_sora_parliament_source_contract_test.py:1356: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

state = '//! This module provides the [`State`] — an in-memory representation of the current blockchain state.\n#![allow(clipp...irect_home_transition};\nuse direct_homes::{ensure_dataspace_classes_preserved, ensure_homed_dataspaces_keep_lanes};\n'

    def require_block_start_enactment_phases(state: str) -> None:
        """Bind original ordered start ownership to bounded borrowed phase helpers."""
        state_path = "crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs"
        wrapper = compact_rust(mask_rust(rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )))
        delegate = (
            "self.block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier("
            "curr_block,None,before_start,after_start)"
        )
        if wrapper.count(delegate) != 1 or not wrapper.endswith("{" + delegate + "}"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must retain their original carrier delegate")
        constructor = rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )
        phases = section(
            constructor,
            "        // Height-trigger: open/close referenda at scheduled heights",
            "        let current_slot =",
            state_path,
        )
        # This small statement region contains only the original height and the
        # four unconditional calls: private-settlement expiry, the SCCP begin-block
        # step (specs/sccp.md S0, before the governance pass), Parliament enactment,
        # then SCCP G4 fast-pause lapse cleanup (§4.7.2, §4.14.7). Ignore line
        # comments, but reject hidden conditions,
        # substituted owners/heights, duplicate calls, or swapped execution order.
        phases = re.sub(r"//[^\n]*", "", phases)
        expected = (
            "let now_h = sb._curr_block.height().get();"
            "Self::apply_block_start_private_settlement_expiry(&mut sb, now_h)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::hook::begin_block(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "Self::apply_block_start_parliament_enactments(&mut sb, now_h)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::pause_lifecycle::lapse_after_enactments(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
        )
        if re.sub(r"\s+", "", phases) != re.sub(r"\s+", "", expected):
>           raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must use the original block and height in order")
E           RuntimeError: crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs: start phases must use the original block and height in order

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

state = '//! This module provides the [`State`] — an in-memory representation of the current blockchain state.\n#![allow(clipp...irect_home_transition};\nuse direct_homes::{ensure_dataspace_classes_preserved, ensure_homed_dataspaces_keep_lanes};\n'

    def require_block_start_enactment_phases(state: str) -> None:
        """Bind original ordered start ownership to bounded borrowed phase helpers."""
        state_path = "crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs"
        wrapper = compact_rust(mask_rust(rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )))
        delegate = (
            "self.block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier("
            "curr_block,None,before_start,after_start)"
        )
        if wrapper.count(delegate) != 1 or not wrapper.endswith("{" + delegate + "}"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must retain their original carrier delegate")
        constructor = rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )
        phases = section(
            constructor,
            "        // Height-trigger: open/close referenda at scheduled heights",
            "        let current_slot =",
            state_path,
        )
        # This small statement region contains only the original height and the
        # four unconditional calls: private-settlement expiry, the SCCP begin-block
        # step (specs/sccp.md S0, before the governance pass), Parliament enactment,
        # then SCCP G4 fast-pause lapse cleanup (§4.7.2, §4.14.7). Ignore line
        # comments, but reject hidden conditions,
        # substituted owners/heights, duplicate calls, or swapped execution order.
        phases = re.sub(r"//[^\n]*", "", phases)
        expected = (
            "let now_h = sb._curr_block.height().get();"
            "Self::apply_block_start_private_settlement_expiry(&mut sb, now_h)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::hook::begin_block(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "Self::apply_block_start_parliament_enactments(&mut sb, now_h)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::pause_lifecycle::lapse_after_enactments(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
        )
        if re.sub(r"\s+", "", phases) != re.sub(r"\s+", "", expected):
>           raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must use the original block and height in order")
E           RuntimeError: crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs: start phases must use the original block and height in order

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

state = '//! This module provides the [`State`] — an in-memory representation of the current blockchain state.\n#![allow(clipp...irect_home_transition};\nuse direct_homes::{ensure_dataspace_classes_preserved, ensure_homed_dataspaces_keep_lanes};\n'

    def require_block_start_enactment_phases(state: str) -> None:
        """Bind original ordered start ownership to bounded borrowed phase helpers."""
        state_path = "crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs"
        wrapper = compact_rust(mask_rust(rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )))
        delegate = (
            "self.block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier("
            "curr_block,None,before_start,after_start)"
        )
        if wrapper.count(delegate) != 1 or not wrapper.endswith("{" + delegate + "}"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must retain their original carrier delegate")
        constructor = rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )
        phases = section(
            constructor,
            "        // Height-trigger: open/close referenda at scheduled heights",
            "        let current_slot =",
            state_path,
        )
        # This small statement region contains only the original height and the
        # four unconditional calls: private-settlement expiry, the SCCP begin-block
        # step (specs/sccp.md S0, before the governance pass), Parliament enactment,
        # then SCCP G4 fast-pause lapse cleanup (§4.7.2, §4.14.7). Ignore line
        # comments, but reject hidden conditions,
        # substituted owners/heights, duplicate calls, or swapped execution order.
        phases = re.sub(r"//[^\n]*", "", phases)
        expected = (
            "let now_h = sb._curr_block.height().get();"
            "Self::apply_block_start_private_settlement_expiry(&mut sb, now_h)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::hook::begin_block(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "Self::apply_block_start_parliament_enactments(&mut sb, now_h)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::pause_lifecycle::lapse_after_enactments(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
        )
        if re.sub(r"\s+", "", phases) != re.sub(r"\s+", "", expected):
>           raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must use the original block and height in order")
E           RuntimeError: crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs: start phases must use the original block and height in order

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

state = '//! This module provides the [`State`] — an in-memory representation of the current blockchain state.\n#![allow(clipp...irect_home_transition};\nuse direct_homes::{ensure_dataspace_classes_preserved, ensure_homed_dataspaces_keep_lanes};\n'

    def require_block_start_enactment_phases(state: str) -> None:
        """Bind original ordered start ownership to bounded borrowed phase helpers."""
        state_path = "crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs"
        wrapper = compact_rust(mask_rust(rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )))
        delegate = (
            "self.block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier("
            "curr_block,None,before_start,after_start)"
        )
        if wrapper.count(delegate) != 1 or not wrapper.endswith("{" + delegate + "}"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must retain their original carrier delegate")
        constructor = rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )
        phases = section(
            constructor,
            "        // Height-trigger: open/close referenda at scheduled heights",
            "        let current_slot =",
            state_path,
        )
        # This small statement region contains only the original height and the
        # four unconditional calls: private-settlement expiry, the SCCP begin-block
        # step (specs/sccp.md S0, before the governance pass), Parliament enactment,
        # then SCCP G4 fast-pause lapse cleanup (§4.7.2, §4.14.7). Ignore line
        # comments, but reject hidden conditions,
        # substituted owners/heights, duplicate calls, or swapped execution order.
        phases = re.sub(r"//[^\n]*", "", phases)
        expected = (
            "let now_h = sb._curr_block.height().get();"
            "Self::apply_block_start_private_settlement_expiry(&mut sb, now_h)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::hook::begin_block(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "Self::apply_block_start_parliament_enactments(&mut sb, now_h)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::pause_lifecycle::lapse_after_enactments(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
        )
        if re.sub(r"\s+", "", phases) != re.sub(r"\s+", "", expected):
>           raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must use the original block and height in order")
E           RuntimeError: crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs: start phases must use the original block and height in order

state = '//! This module provides the [`State`] — an in-memory representation of the current blockchain state.\n#![allow(clipp...irect_home_transition};\nuse direct_homes::{ensure_dataspace_classes_preserved, ensure_homed_dataspaces_keep_lanes};\n'

    def require_block_start_enactment_phases(state: str) -> None:
        """Bind original ordered start ownership to bounded borrowed phase helpers."""
        state_path = "crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs"
        wrapper = compact_rust(mask_rust(rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )))
        delegate = (
            "self.block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier("
            "curr_block,None,before_start,after_start)"
        )
        if wrapper.count(delegate) != 1 or not wrapper.endswith("{" + delegate + "}"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must retain their original carrier delegate")
        constructor = rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )
        phases = section(
            constructor,
            "        // Height-trigger: open/close referenda at scheduled heights",
            "        let current_slot =",
            state_path,
        )
        # This small statement region contains only the original height and the
        # four unconditional calls: private-settlement expiry, the SCCP begin-block
        # step (specs/sccp.md S0, before the governance pass), Parliament enactment,
        # then SCCP G4 fast-pause lapse cleanup (§4.7.2, §4.14.7). Ignore line
        # comments, but reject hidden conditions,
        # substituted owners/heights, duplicate calls, or swapped execution order.
        phases = re.sub(r"//[^\n]*", "", phases)
        expected = (
            "let now_h = sb._curr_block.height().get();"
            "Self::apply_block_start_private_settlement_expiry(&mut sb, now_h)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::hook::begin_block(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "Self::apply_block_start_parliament_enactments(&mut sb, now_h)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::pause_lifecycle::lapse_after_enactments(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
        )
        if re.sub(r"\s+", "", phases) != re.sub(r"\s+", "", expected):
>           raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must use the original block and height in order")
E           RuntimeError: crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs: start phases must use the original block and height in order

            raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must retain their original carrier delegate")
        constructor = rust_item(
            state,

            "Self::apply_block_start_private_settlement_expiry(&mut sb, now_h)"

        "missing_expiry", "missing_enactment", "duplicate", "swapped",
        "wrong_height", "conditional", "late_before_flag", "late_after_execution",
        "missing_sccp_begin", "duplicate_sccp_begin", "sccp_begin_after_enactment",
        "conditional_sccp_begin", "missing_sccp_lapse", "duplicate_sccp_lapse",
        "sccp_lapse_before_enactment", "conditional_sccp_lapse", "foreign_sccp_lapse",
    ))
    def test_block_start_phase_calls_reject_disconnected_or_late_owners(mutation: str) -> None:
        """Valid Rust statement changes cannot disconnect helpers or defer them until after execution."""
        source = guard.read(STATE_PATH)
>       guard.require_block_start_enactment_phases(source)

scripts/tests/check_sora_parliament_source_contract_test.py:1389: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

state = '//! This module provides the [`State`] — an in-memory representation of the current blockchain state.\n#![allow(clipp...irect_home_transition};\nuse direct_homes::{ensure_dataspace_classes_preserved, ensure_homed_dataspaces_keep_lanes};\n'

    def require_block_start_enactment_phases(state: str) -> None:
        """Bind original ordered start ownership to bounded borrowed phase helpers."""
        state_path = "crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs"
        wrapper = compact_rust(mask_rust(rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )))
        delegate = (
            "self.block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier("
            "curr_block,None,before_start,after_start)"
        )
        if wrapper.count(delegate) != 1 or not wrapper.endswith("{" + delegate + "}"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must retain their original carrier delegate")
        constructor = rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )
        phases = section(
            constructor,
            "        // Height-trigger: open/close referenda at scheduled heights",
            "        let current_slot =",
            state_path,
        )
        # This small statement region contains only the original height and the
        # four unconditional calls: private-settlement expiry, the SCCP begin-block
        # step (specs/sccp.md S0, before the governance pass), Parliament enactment,
        # then SCCP G4 fast-pause lapse cleanup (§4.7.2, §4.14.7). Ignore line
        # comments, but reject hidden conditions,
        # substituted owners/heights, duplicate calls, or swapped execution order.
        phases = re.sub(r"//[^\n]*", "", phases)
        expected = (
            "let now_h = sb._curr_block.height().get();"
            "Self::apply_block_start_private_settlement_expiry(&mut sb, now_h)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::hook::begin_block(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "Self::apply_block_start_parliament_enactments(&mut sb, now_h)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::pause_lifecycle::lapse_after_enactments(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
        )
        if re.sub(r"\s+", "", phases) != re.sub(r"\s+", "", expected):
>           raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must use the original block and height in order")
E           RuntimeError: crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs: start phases must use the original block and height in order

scripts/formal/check_sora_parliament_source_contract.py:1539: RuntimeError
_ test_block_start_phase_bodies_reject_changed_height_or_rollback[barrier.manifest.expiry_height < now_h-barrier.manifest.expiry_height <= now_h] _

original = 'barrier.manifest.expiry_height < now_h'
replacement = 'barrier.manifest.expiry_height <= now_h'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("original,replacement", (
        ("barrier.manifest.expiry_height < now_h", "barrier.manifest.expiry_height <= now_h"),
        ("let mut expiry = sb.try_transaction()?;", "let mut expiry = sb.try_transaction().unwrap();"),
        ("expiry.apply();", "drop(expiry);"),
        ("*enact_at_height < now_h", "*enact_at_height > now_h"),
        (".get(&now_h)", ".get(&(now_h + 1))"),
        ("drop(enactment);", "enactment.apply();"),
        ("let mut enactment = sb.try_transaction()?;", "let mut enactment = sb.try_transaction().unwrap();"),
        ("let mut failure = sb.try_transaction()?;", "let mut failure = sb.try_transaction().unwrap();"),
        ("failure.apply();", "drop(failure);"),
        ("let due_parliament_certificates = sb", "let _ = sb.world.parliament_attempts.iter();\n        let due_parliament_certificates = sb"),
    ))
    def test_block_start_phase_bodies_reject_changed_height_or_rollback(
        original: str, replacement: str,
    ) -> None:
        """Both helpers retain exact due selection, actual rollback, and terminal recording."""
        source = guard.read(STATE_PATH)
>       guard.require_block_start_enactment_phases(source)

        ("barrier.manifest.expiry_height < now_h", "barrier.manifest.expiry_height <= now_h"),
        ("let mut expiry = sb.try_transaction()?;", "let mut expiry = sb.try_transaction().unwrap();"),
        ("expiry.apply();", "drop(expiry);"),
        ("*enact_at_height < now_h", "*enact_at_height > now_h"),
        (".get(&now_h)", ".get(&(now_h + 1))"),
        ("drop(enactment);", "enactment.apply();"),
        ("let mut enactment = sb.try_transaction()?;", "let mut enactment = sb.try_transaction().unwrap();"),
        ("let mut failure = sb.try_transaction()?;", "let mut failure = sb.try_transaction().unwrap();"),
        ("failure.apply();", "drop(failure);"),
        ("let due_parliament_certificates = sb", "let _ = sb.world.parliament_attempts.iter();\n        let due_parliament_certificates = sb"),
    ))
    def test_block_start_phase_bodies_reject_changed_height_or_rollback(
        original: str, replacement: str,
    ) -> None:
        """Both helpers retain exact due selection, actual rollback, and terminal recording."""
        source = guard.read(STATE_PATH)
>       guard.require_block_start_enactment_phases(source)

state = '//! This module provides the [`State`] — an in-memory representation of the current blockchain state.\n#![allow(clipp...irect_home_transition};\nuse direct_homes::{ensure_dataspace_classes_preserved, ensure_homed_dataspaces_keep_lanes};\n'

    def require_block_start_enactment_phases(state: str) -> None:
        """Bind original ordered start ownership to bounded borrowed phase helpers."""
        state_path = "crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs"
        wrapper = compact_rust(mask_rust(rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )))
        delegate = (
            "self.block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier("
            "curr_block,None,before_start,after_start)"
        )
        if wrapper.count(delegate) != 1 or not wrapper.endswith("{" + delegate + "}"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must retain their original carrier delegate")
        constructor = rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )
        phases = section(
            constructor,
            "        // Height-trigger: open/close referenda at scheduled heights",
            "        let current_slot =",
            state_path,
        )
        # This small statement region contains only the original height and the
        # four unconditional calls: private-settlement expiry, the SCCP begin-block
        # step (specs/sccp.md S0, before the governance pass), Parliament enactment,
        # then SCCP G4 fast-pause lapse cleanup (§4.7.2, §4.14.7). Ignore line
        # comments, but reject hidden conditions,
        # substituted owners/heights, duplicate calls, or swapped execution order.
        phases = re.sub(r"//[^\n]*", "", phases)
        expected = (
            "let now_h = sb._curr_block.height().get();"
            "Self::apply_block_start_private_settlement_expiry(&mut sb, now_h)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::hook::begin_block(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "Self::apply_block_start_parliament_enactments(&mut sb, now_h)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::pause_lifecycle::lapse_after_enactments(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
        )
        if re.sub(r"\s+", "", phases) != re.sub(r"\s+", "", expected):
>           raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must use the original block and height in order")
E           RuntimeError: crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs: start phases must use the original block and height in order

state = '//! This module provides the [`State`] — an in-memory representation of the current blockchain state.\n#![allow(clipp...irect_home_transition};\nuse direct_homes::{ensure_dataspace_classes_preserved, ensure_homed_dataspaces_keep_lanes};\n'

    def require_block_start_enactment_phases(state: str) -> None:
        """Bind original ordered start ownership to bounded borrowed phase helpers."""
        state_path = "crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs"
        wrapper = compact_rust(mask_rust(rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )))
        delegate = (
            "self.block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier("
            "curr_block,None,before_start,after_start)"
        )
        if wrapper.count(delegate) != 1 or not wrapper.endswith("{" + delegate + "}"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must retain their original carrier delegate")
        constructor = rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )
        phases = section(
            constructor,
            "        // Height-trigger: open/close referenda at scheduled heights",
            "        let current_slot =",
            state_path,
        )
        # This small statement region contains only the original height and the
        # four unconditional calls: private-settlement expiry, the SCCP begin-block
        # step (specs/sccp.md S0, before the governance pass), Parliament enactment,
        # then SCCP G4 fast-pause lapse cleanup (§4.7.2, §4.14.7). Ignore line
        # comments, but reject hidden conditions,
        # substituted owners/heights, duplicate calls, or swapped execution order.
        phases = re.sub(r"//[^\n]*", "", phases)
        expected = (
            "let now_h = sb._curr_block.height().get();"
            "Self::apply_block_start_private_settlement_expiry(&mut sb, now_h)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::hook::begin_block(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "Self::apply_block_start_parliament_enactments(&mut sb, now_h)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::pause_lifecycle::lapse_after_enactments(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
        )
        if re.sub(r"\s+", "", phases) != re.sub(r"\s+", "", expected):
>           raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must use the original block and height in order")

state = '//! This module provides the [`State`] — an in-memory representation of the current blockchain state.\n#![allow(clipp...irect_home_transition};\nuse direct_homes::{ensure_dataspace_classes_preserved, ensure_homed_dataspaces_keep_lanes};\n'

    def require_block_start_enactment_phases(state: str) -> None:
        """Bind original ordered start ownership to bounded borrowed phase helpers."""
        state_path = "crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs"
        wrapper = compact_rust(mask_rust(rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )))
        delegate = (
            "self.block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier("
            "curr_block,None,before_start,after_start)"
        )
        if wrapper.count(delegate) != 1 or not wrapper.endswith("{" + delegate + "}"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must retain their original carrier delegate")
        constructor = rust_item(
            state,
            "    fn block_with_owned_start_stages_with_carrier<'state, E: std::fmt::Debug, T, R>(",
            state_path,
        )
        phases = section(
            constructor,
            "        // Height-trigger: open/close referenda at scheduled heights",
            "        let current_slot =",
            state_path,
        )
        # This small statement region contains only the original height and the
        # four unconditional calls: private-settlement expiry, the SCCP begin-block
        # step (specs/sccp.md S0, before the governance pass), Parliament enactment,
        # then SCCP G4 fast-pause lapse cleanup (§4.7.2, §4.14.7). Ignore line
        # comments, but reject hidden conditions,
        # substituted owners/heights, duplicate calls, or swapped execution order.
        phases = re.sub(r"//[^\n]*", "", phases)
        expected = (
            "let now_h = sb._curr_block.height().get();"
            "Self::apply_block_start_private_settlement_expiry(&mut sb, now_h)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::hook::begin_block(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
            "Self::apply_block_start_parliament_enactments(&mut sb, now_h)?;"
            "crate::smartcontracts::isi::sccp::pause_lifecycle::lapse_after_enactments(&mut sb)"
            ".map_err(StateBlockStartError::Storage)?;"
        )
        if re.sub(r"\s+", "", phases) != re.sub(r"\s+", "", expected):
>           raise RuntimeError(f"{state_path}: start phases must use the original block and height in order")
E           RuntimeError: crates/iroha_core/src/state.rs: start phases must use the original block and height in order

        path: str, old: str, new: str, message: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        """Negative source mutations of the pins bound inside the production entrypoint."""
        original_read = guard.read
        source = original_read(path)
        if old:
            assert source.count(old) >= 1, old
            mutated = source.replace(old, new, 1)
        else:
            mutated = source + new
        assert mutated != source
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            guard, "read", lambda relative: mutated if relative == path else original_read(relative)
        )

    def test_production_checker_enforces_pulse_isolation_state_and_model_pins(
        path: str, old: str, new: str, message: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        """Negative source mutations of the pins bound inside the production entrypoint."""
        original_read = guard.read
        source = original_read(path)
        if old:
            assert source.count(old) >= 1, old
            mutated = source.replace(old, new, 1)
        else:
            mutated = source + new
        assert mutated != source
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            guard, "read", lambda relative: mutated if relative == path else original_read(relative)
        )

    def test_production_checker_enforces_pulse_isolation_state_and_model_pins(
        path: str, old: str, new: str, message: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        """Negative source mutations of the pins bound inside the production entrypoint."""
        original_read = guard.read
        source = original_read(path)
        if old:
            assert source.count(old) >= 1, old
            mutated = source.replace(old, new, 1)
        else:
            mutated = source + new
        assert mutated != source
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            guard, "read", lambda relative: mutated if relative == path else original_read(relative)
        )

    ) -> None:
        """Negative source mutations of the pins bound inside the production entrypoint."""

    def test_production_checker_enforces_pulse_isolation_state_and_model_pins(
        path: str, old: str, new: str, message: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        """Negative source mutations of the pins bound inside the production entrypoint."""
        original_read = guard.read
        source = original_read(path)
        if old:
            assert source.count(old) >= 1, old
            mutated = source.replace(old, new, 1)
        else:
            mutated = source + new
        assert mutated != source
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            guard, "read", lambda relative: mutated if relative == path else original_read(relative)
        )

    def test_production_checker_enforces_pulse_isolation_state_and_model_pins(
        path: str, old: str, new: str, message: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        """Negative source mutations of the pins bound inside the production entrypoint."""
        original_read = guard.read
        source = original_read(path)
        if old:
            assert source.count(old) >= 1, old
            mutated = source.replace(old, new, 1)
        else:
            mutated = source + new
        assert mutated != source
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            guard, "read", lambda relative: mutated if relative == path else original_read(relative)
        )

        path: str, old: str, new: str, message: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        """Negative source mutations of the pins bound inside the production entrypoint."""
        original_read = guard.read
        source = original_read(path)
        if old:
            assert source.count(old) >= 1, old
            mutated = source.replace(old, new, 1)
        else:
            mutated = source + new
        assert mutated != source
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            guard, "read", lambda relative: mutated if relative == path else original_read(relative)
        )

    def test_production_checker_enforces_pulse_isolation_state_and_model_pins(
        path: str, old: str, new: str, message: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        """Negative source mutations of the pins bound inside the production entrypoint."""
        original_read = guard.read
        source = original_read(path)
        if old:
            assert source.count(old) >= 1, old
            mutated = source.replace(old, new, 1)
        else:
            mutated = source + new
        assert mutated != source
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            guard, "read", lambda relative: mutated if relative == path else original_read(relative)
        )
